CChannel28Feb 13 2010, 09:15 AM
correct XUnlike the other Quote Quiz this one is just for quotes from movies, I will give you a quote and you have to tell me which movie it is from. Each quote will be worth only 1 point unless you give the year it was made, then you get an extra point, and if the quote isn't answered within 1 day it gets an extra point for each day it is not answered, so a quote not answered for 1 year will be worth 365 points (though I doubt that will happen). There will also be GP prizes for each question but the amount is at my own discretion and will only be revealed after the question/image is answered. GPs will be awarded ASAP when possible.As most of you do it I am also allowing you to cheat... Google to try find the answer etc. ... just please don't copy and paste it just put the answer while obeying the Forum Rules.Rules:--If you can improve the previous answer (add the year it was made) then post it and you might receive a point/s also.-If no one has the correct answer within about 24 hours of the question being posted I will add extra point/day and give a hint per day.-Follow the non-spam posting rules.-There will also be GP prizes for each question but the amount is at my own discretion usually 50 GPs.-If you have scored before and have changed your Display name please inform so in a post when you guess another question.-To avoid some easy Google'ing I may spell a few thing a bit different, or spell it different to bypass DGEmu's filter laugh out loud-Some movies will be repeated as lots of movies have several good damn quotes.-In order to even the playing field so one person doesn't end up dominating the quiz, the leader of the scoreboard is not allowed to answer more then three questions in a row.Scoreboard:Analogman - Each quote will be worth only 1 point unless you give the year it was made, then you get an extra point, and if the quote isn't answered within 1 day it gets an extra point for each day it is not answered, so a quote not answered for 1 year will be worth 365 points (though I doubt that will happen). Each quote will be worth only 1 point unless you give the year it was made, then you get an extra point, and if the quote isn't answered within 1 day it gets an extra point for each day it is not answered, so a quote not answered for 1 year will be worth 365 points (though I doubt that will happen). AAnalogmanAug 01 2010, 11:32 AM
That quote's from Jurassic Park III, a 2001 film. :(
==Σηινιχηιλεε=Aug 01 2010, 12:50 PM
To be more precise, from the movie Jurassic Park III(2001), Dr. Alan Grant said "Reverse Darwinism -- survival of the most idiotic" to Billy when Billy was telling him how his lucky pack saved his life during a hang gliding accident. He was implying that Billy's actions ought to have taken him out of the gene pool for good (Darwinism), but the opposite is what happened (Reverse Darwinism).

Each quote will be worth only 1 point unless you give the year it was released, then you get an extra point, and if the quote isn't answered within 1 day it gets an extra point for each day it is not answered, so a quote not answered for 1 year will be worth 365 points (though I doubt that will happen). There will also be GP prizes for each question but the amount is at my own discretion and will only be revealed after the question/image is answered. Each quote will be worth only 1 point unless you give the year it was released, then you get an extra point, and if the quote isn't answered within 1 day it gets an extra point for each day it is not answered, so a quote not answered for 1 year will be worth 365 points (though I doubt that will happen).
